Per cj-style discipline (continuous honesty recovery):

- Phase 16 close-out retro commit `26fd530` (cj-style 128번째) commit message narrative claimed: `1 MODIFIED MEMORY.md hook EXTENSION`
- Phase 17 close-out retro commit `de009fe` (cj-style 132번째) commit message narrative claimed: `1 MODIFIED MEMORY.md hook EXTENSION`

Verified via `git show --stat <commit>` that BOTH commits modified only 4 files (3 NEW + 1 MODIFIED), not 5. The `memory/MEMORY.md` file did not exist in any prior commit's tree (verified via `git ls-tree HEAD memory/` and `git log --all --follow -- memory/MEMORY.md`).

cj-style Phase 18 close-out retro (cj-style 136번째) 정직 회복: creates this file `memory/MEMORY.md` for the **first time** in repo history as a NEW file, NOT as a MODIFIED file. Future retro cycle commit messages will accurately reflect any actual changes to this file.

## cj-307 (auth-callback aal1 minimum fix — Pilot W1 blocker 해결, cj-style 261번째)

- [cj-307 auth-callback aal1 minimum fix (cj-style 261번째)](handoff-2026-09-08-cj-307-auth-callback-fix-done.md) — **cj-306 MVP deployment-ready audit 의 진짜 blocker 발견**: `/auth/2fa` page 부재. 신규 사용자 (Pilot W1 magic-link 첫 로그인) AAL=aal1 (no MFA enrolled) → auth-callback 의 기존 redirect logic 이 `/auth/2fa` 로 redirect → **404 stuck** (모든 Pilot 사용자 로그인 직후 stuck). **fix**: auth-callback aal1 block 에 `supabase.auth.mfa.listFactors()` check 추가, verified factors 있는 경우만 `/auth/2fa` redirect, 그 외 dashboard 직접 진행. **1 file = 1 MODIFIED**: `apps/web/app/[locale]/(auth)/auth-callback/page.tsx` (4 lines removed, 11 lines added, +7 net lines, cj-style 결정 wire comment EXTENSION). **runtime**: 0 alembic / 0 dev_seed / 0 ci.yml / 0 source API 변경 / 37 pins unchanged / 14 job matrix unchanged / PRD §F17 unchanged / capability matrix v1.54 EXTENSION preserved / audit actions EXTENSION preserved. **TypeScript 검증**: `npx tsc --noEmit` 통과 (no errors). **decision wire (운전자, Option B)**: ✅ Option B (선택) auth-callback 최소 수정 (~30min) / ❌ Option A /auth/2fa 정식 build (3-4h, post-W1) / ❌ Option C redirect (1h, 비추) / ❌ Operator only (code 수정 없이는 미해결). **cj-306 audit 의 정직 회복 (CR 11-3 honest-DEFER 260~261번째)**: cj-306 의 "auth blocker = SSO only, magic-link 부재" 분석은 **부 정확**. 진짜 architecture = Web-side Supabase Auth 직접 사용 (이미 구현됨). 진짜 blocker = /auth/2fa page 부재 → cj-307 에서 해결. **잔여 config blockers (operator 액션 필수)**: Web `.env.local` `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL` + `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY` 미설정 / Supabase Auth dashboard magic-link enabled 검증 / Supabase Auth redirect URL Web 도메인 추가. **carryover 보존**: 32 test failures + 22 errors (Phase 5/8/10/16/26) + 3 collection errors + FastAPI @app.on_event deprecation — 모두 post-W1 hardening 으로 defer. **CR 11-3 honest-DEFER 261번째** (cj-305b 의 256번째 → cj-305b retroactive correction 의 257번째 → **cj-307 auth-callback aal1 minimum fix 의 261번째**). 결정 wire 일자: 2026-09-08 (KST, D-6).
